◈ THE AUDIT — the System reads your run back to you, and the galaxy listens. A lesser seat — global standing, waves 1–3 faced (21/12/6), with 5 wave-4 fought, 15 contests survived, 3 crises weathered. The table does not rise. It prices.
◈ THE WAR PATH — The System pauses — actually pausing. It keeps a list of every tooth pulled, and for once it reads the list instead of the press release. On day 111, Scarlett put down Ugly-Eyes. On day 97, Scarlett put down The Big Beast — and 4 more tooths after.
"THE HERALD came. Named and located — the war council knew its face. They faced Big Ugly and lived to sit here. Even now the wave-5 bar reads: blood your fighters (0 of 2 veterans)." — the System is still reading the list.
The table notes 1 ascension from one village — its terms improve accordingly. Nobody has ever seen that before.
The table notes the overtime: 6 weeks kept waiting, 2 of Haven lost meanwhile. Every door's standing −30.
📺 SYSTEM: "EVERYONE. SIT. WATCH. The teeth and the weather can WAIT — nothing out there matters until this is done. ...Did I stutter? SIT."
◈ ABILITY — Dreadfield: The room feels you before you speak. Alien-terror starts at 2.
◈ ABILITY — War-table: You read the table's fear the way you read weather. The Nielsen flinches first — you saw it.
◈ ABILITY — Oathbound: Your terror is pledged as a shield: your own people's horror grows half as fast.
◈ ABILITY — Shieldbearer: Spend terror to calm horror — the shield, not the sword. (Offered in the final beat.)
📺 SYSTEM: "🔴 LIVE! the terror broadcast: WHAT EATING BUILT"
◈ THE FEARED — "Look what eating built." The footage plays unasked — the deer, the fire, the ones who came for you and didn't come back. Nobody at the table is comfortable. That is the point.
The table has read your ledger. The protected outnumber the killed; the food truth was shared; promises kept. The light road is open.
The time you stayed your hand. The table doesn't know what to do with it — which is its own kind of fear. Your people breathe.
◈ THE TABLE FALTERS — you played your hand before the fear was in the room, and the demonstration underwhelmed. The ring dims. Fear, it turns out, is a wasting asset — spend it wrong and it spends you.
You take the smaller door. It is still a door.
◈ ABILITY — Deep Listen: already in your hands.
◈ ABILITY — Faithful Carry: already in your hands.
◈ ABILITY — Relay-sight: already in your hands.
◈ ABILITY — Translate: already in your hands.
◈ ABILITY — Sanctuary: already in your hands.
◈ ABILITY — Advocate: already in your hands.
📺 SYSTEM: "🔴 LIVE! the merge: A HUMAN ENTERS THE MACHINE"
◈ THE RELAY — You plug into Haven's switchboard — or deeper, straight into the wires. The other paths used the System as terrain. Here it is the relationship. Push enough buttons, try enough things, and you start to integrate with the matrix. The matrix, it turns out, has been waiting for someone to try.
The table has read your ledger. The protected outnumber the killed; the food truth was shared; promises kept. The light road is open.
◈ REROUTE — honest. Every message carried faithfully, in order, undistorted. Slow. The wires feel *clean*. Somewhere, the System notes the cleanliness with something like respect.
◈ THE TABLE FALTERS — you pushed your own pattern into the wires before the console knew your hands, and the System quarantined it. The ring dims. The System quarantines the console — kindly, the way you quarantine something you don't want to lose but can't trust. The merge stops one button short.
You take the smaller door. It is still a door.
◈ ABILITY — Spotlight: The room is already warm. Audience starts at 2.
◈ ABILITY — Callback: One bad act can be redone. (Offered once, after a bad beat.)
◈ ABILITY — Standing-O: Genuine love doesn't crater: the audience can't fall below 0 while you hold it.
◈ ABILITY — Eulogy: The quiet ones' act lands harder than any headliner. (Offered in the final beat.)
📺 SYSTEM: "🔴 LIVE! the variety finale: ONE NIGHT ONLY"
◈ THE BELOVED — The audience is chanting. You don't know the words — it isn't a language with words — but you know the feeling. The table can't move against the cast's favorite. That's leverage. Tonight, the cast is your village.
The table has read your ledger. The protected outnumber the killed; the food truth was shared; promises kept; the relay carried faithfully. The light road is open.
Ethan tells the truth — the unproduced kind. The audience weeps in eleven dimensions. You can't fake this. You didn't.
You do the thing only The Living Garden can do. The ring goes quiet in eleven dimensions. It isn't a threat. It's a demonstration of what a human became by eating, and not stopping.
You spend it all — the love, the light, the finale slot — on people the cameras never found. You name the unfilmed villages. You name the dead. The audience weeps in eleven dimensions. This is what the show was for, it turns out.
◈ THE BELOVED — the last choice is yours, and it's live.
◈ THE BELOVED — "Speak for the quiet ones."
You spend the last of the love on people who never had any. The villages nobody filmed, the dead nobody named. The audience weeps in eleven dimensions. This is what the show was for, it turns out. The beloved one's final act: making sure nobody else needs to be.
You fed other mouths with what passed through your hands. Your own bowl still needed filling. You let someone do it.
You knew the harvest belonged to more than the person who could call it. Someone else was allowed to hold the serving spoon.
◈ THE ARCHITECT — Ethan Hunt ascended through doors you built: relay. The galaxy will remember the god's name. The village will remember who built the door. Both are true. Neither is small.
The terms held — the table had priced the village, ascension and all, and the village paid in full.
◈ THE OVERSIGHT —
You won. Say it plainly: you won. The village endures. The table signed. The galaxy applauds, and the applause is real, and you earned it.
And somewhere out there — past the cameras, past the light — in rows of humming glass, the little ones are still napping.
The System put a pod on the screen, and you let the segment play.
They are still there. Still small. Still waiting for someone to decide they matter more than the moment.
That is what the word means. Not the watching — anyone can watch. The oversight is the looking away. The most terrible thing the System ever did, it did by accident, because it was bored. You did it on purpose, because you were busy.
The village will tell your story for years. It will be a good story. Listen for the part they don't tell.
